# Proof packet formalization progress

The active scope is every claimed theorem in the delivered packet index.
A packet is complete when its source claims have explicit object-language
statements, checked derivations in `Prf PRAAx`, and documented assumptions
and encoding conventions. The table below indexes the whole packet scope
and the current derivation families. Each module records the mathematical
statements it proves. Source-claim reconciliation remains open across the
packet inventory.
The [complete FIBER claim audit](FIBER-CLAIMS.md) covers its theorem
environments, target signatures, implementation phases and regression
obligations, with exact statement-level status.
The [finite-demand claim audit](FINITE-DEMAND-CLAIMS.md) accounts for all 17
source theorem environments and the exact scheduling/aggregate/root/norm
producer boundaries.
The [Minkowski claim audit](MINKOWSKI-CLAIMS.md) records the complete
rational-box certificate theorem and finite-prefix pigeonhole lemma, and
distinguishes the remaining polytope/volume, equality and real-sign claims.
The [Gamma claim audit](GAMMA-CLAIMS.md) retains all 62 source-ledger families
and distinguishes finite factor/tail names from the remaining analytic endpoints.

The packet itself uses only this rational version ("If uniformity is also specified on all constructive real inputs, the same estimate extends to them by approximation. Only the rational version is used in the proof."); the optional extension to real inputs and the rational affine rescaling of an `n`-cube of `ℝ^n` onto `C_n` are not formalized. |

The declarations belong to the corresponding namespaces under
`LeanProofs.IPRA.Combinatorics`, `LeanProofs.IPRA.Analysis`, and
`LeanProofs.IPRA.Foundations`. The module links in the table below locate
their full formulas and proofs.

The Barnes packet still requires the analytic canonical-product construction,
complex functional and reflection identities, multiplication formulas,
analytic special values, asymptotic estimates and characterizations. Its
integer product results provide a finite part of those arguments. The FIBER
packet still requires the remaining source-node compiler adapters and cyclic unrolling, cluster and finite-algebra
observation proofs, concrete application instances, and the adapters recorded
in its claim audit. The full source scope remains active.


## Source statements needing correction

- In finite-use-v2 `thm:nested-name`, nesting and shrinking diameters give
  a regular center name. Equality with a particular admissible solution also
  needs its membership in every isolating region, or a theorem locating it
  there. For example, the target set `{0, 1/8}` and balls centered at zero
  of radius `2^(-k-2)` meet the displayed nesting, nonemptiness and local
  diameter conditions. Both solutions belong to the initial ball. The
  solution `1/8` lies outside later balls and differs from the center name.
  The source's global assertion needs that extra compatibility hypothesis.
  [Source statement](../docs/proofs/foundations/ipra_finite_use_framework_2026-11-15_v2/sections/09_compactness.tex)
- In Barnes `eq:product-cutoff`, the displayed cutoff using
  `ceil(2^(p+5) R³ / 3)` yields `B_N ≤ 2^(-p-4)` for
  `B_N = 2 R³ / (3N)`. The following sentence claims `2^(-p-5)`.
  At `R = 1`, `p = 0`, the displayed cutoff is `N = 11`, and
  `B_N = 2/33 > 1/32`. Using `2^(p+6)` in the cutoff supplies the claimed
  bound. The underlying `eq:qtail` bound has its stated constant.
  [Source cutoff](../docs/proofs/analysis/barnes_g_ipra_2026-11-16/paper/sections/03_canonical_product.tex)

- In FIBER Section 8, “Gap-guarded labeling,” a positive distance gap
  between complex-root clusters does not make lexicographic box order
  independent of the certificate. The roots `i` and `−i` have gap `2`;
  arbitrarily small rational boxes containing them can have their real
  coordinates perturbed in either order. Real-coordinate ties require an
  additional order-separation or locator convention. Metric separation alone
  does give unique geometric matching at sufficiently fine precision.
  [Source proposition](../docs/proofs/foundations/fiber_ipra_2026-11-16/paper/sections/08_algebra.tex)

## Packet inventory

The inventory contains 155 packet entries, including all 115 original entries.
The derivations README links proof families to the source packets.
Completion requires reconciling all theorems within each packet, including
claims beyond those families.

`Geometry/MorleyCertificate` and the Type 15 part of `Geometry/PentagonLedger` currently provide symbolic polynomial certificates, not non-vacuous encoded geometry. Their ordinary iPRA formulas use integer variables, and antecedents such as `2*u_c=1`, `rho^2=3` or `h^2=3` have no integer instance. Scalar-quotient or quadratic-ring realizations and the original geometry checkers remain open. The conditional integer ledgers and area identities retain their stated finite algebraic scope.

F02 (for coded topology tables), F08 and F24 are in [Topology/FiniteRecovery](Lean/IPRADerivations/Topology/FiniteRecovery.lean). F18 necessity and F20 (Boolean subsets) are in [Topology/FiniteNormalityConverse](Lean/IPRADerivations/Topology/FiniteNormalityConverse.lean), so all of F01–F24 are derived. In the algebraic section, A03 (finite Euler–Poincaré, in every commutative ring and sign-free for iPRA terms), A06 (Euler additivity) and the Euler half of A05 (collapse) are in [Topology/EulerCharacteristic](Lean/IPRADerivations/Topology/EulerCharacteristic.lean). A01 (`∂² = 0`, by the sign-reversing pairing of deletion orders) is in [Topology/BoundarySquare](Lean/IPRADerivations/Topology/BoundarySquare.lean). A09 and A10 (greedy and two-colourings, the odd-cycle certificate) are in [Topology/TwoColoring](Lean/IPRADerivations/Topology/TwoColoring.lean), A12 and A14 for function sheaves in [Topology/FiniteSheaves](Lean/IPRADerivations/Topology/FiniteSheaves.lean), A11 (the canonical-cover test), A13, A14 for arbitrary sheaves, A15 (stalkwise isomorphisms) and A16 (local subobjects and equalizers) for arbitrary presheaf tables in [Topology/FiniteSheafTheory](Lean/IPRADerivations/Topology/FiniteSheafTheory.lean), A17 and A18 in [Topology/BooleanAtoms](Lean/IPRADerivations/Topology/BooleanAtoms.lean) (these four for coded finite data), and the cone identity of A20 in [Topology/SimplicialCone](Lean/IPRADerivations/Topology/SimplicialCone.lean). D02 (cubical Sperner parity, the boundary rules `v_i = 0 → λ(v) < i`, `v_i = N → λ(v) ≠ i − 1`) is `sperner_cube` of [Topology/SpernerCube](Lean/IPRADerivations/Topology/SpernerCube.lean) for every dimension, and D03 (separated threshold arithmetic: two grids of different shifts do not both have a boundary in one unit interval) is [Topology/StaggeredGrids](Lean/IPRADerivations/Topology/StaggeredGrids.lean), both derived for `dimension_invariance_ipra` (checked against the statements). The remaining metric, algebraic (A02, the rank count of A04, the Betti half of A05, A07, A08, A19 and the carrier homotopy of A20), sphere (S03, the remaining S04–S06 assembly) results are not yet derived. The finite estimates of S04–S06 (the S04 chain argument after the carrier lemma, the S05 labels, and the S06 scan, norm, Lipschitz and normalization bounds) are iPRA derivations over integer terms in [Topology/SphereEstimates](Lean/IPRADerivations/Topology/SphereEstimates.lean), and L03, L04 also have iPRA forms over integer terms in [Topology/LimitCertificates](Lean/IPRADerivations/Topology/LimitCertificates.lean) (`center_interior_ipra`, `dual_bound_ipra`, `selected_vanish_ipra`). L01, L02 and L05 are in [Topology/FiniteLimits](Lean/IPRADerivations/Topology/FiniteLimits.lean), and L03 (the interior step of convex loops), L04 (the rational disk-intersection certificate) and L06 (an exact union extractor would decide halting) are in [Topology/LimitCertificates](Lean/IPRADerivations/Topology/LimitCertificates.lean), so all of L01–L06 are derived. In the metric section, the geometric tail and coordinate bounds of the weighted product metric (`eq:product-tail`, `eq:coordinate-bound`, the net error of `thm:countable-net-product`) and the local-gap Urysohn estimate (`prop:local-urysohn`) are in [Topology/MetricProducts](Lean/IPRADerivations/Topology/MetricProducts.lean), cleared of dyadic denominators. The approximate extrema of `cor:approximate-extrema` and the upper endpoint of the certified interval of `thm:computable-uniform-metric` are in [Topology/ApproxExtrema](Lean/IPRADerivations/Topology/ApproxExtrema.lean). The explicit Hilbert-cube embedding `thm:dense-hilbert-embedding` (truncated coordinates are `1`-Lipschitz, the embedding is `1`-Lipschitz for the weighted metric, and the inverse local modulus `eq:dense-embedding-inverse`) is in [Topology/HilbertEmbedding](Lean/IPRADerivations/Topology/HilbertEmbedding.lean). The finite cores of the compact-open sandwich `eq:compact-open-sandwich`, evaluation `prop:metric-evaluation`, the converse of `thm:metric-currying` and the inverse neighbourhood transformation of `thm:coordinate-metrization` are in [Topology/MetricNeighborhoods](Lean/IPRADerivations/Topology/MetricNeighborhoods.lean). `prop:euclidean-open-cube` (`h(t) = t/(1+|t|)` is `1`-Lipschitz, `h⁻¹` is `δ^{−2}`-Lipschitz on `|s| ≤ 1 − δ`, and the maps are inverse) is derived in [Topology/OpenCubeHomeo](Lean/IPRADerivations/Topology/OpenCubeHomeo.lean). S01 (the cross-polytope cycle: `∂ z_n = 0`, `A_# z_n = (−1)^{n+1} z_n`, `z_n ≠ 0`) is in [Topology/SphereCycle](Lean/IPRADerivations/Topology/SphereCycle.lean). |

The cylinder algebra, the completed order and linearity on names, Vitali completion, the passage of the martingale rate to names (its finite block-cleared core `‖C X − X‖₁ ≤ 2‖X − x‖₁` is `martingale_rate` of [Probability/MartingaleRate](Lean/IPRADerivations/Probability/MartingaleRate.lean), and the bounded stopping theorem `E M_τ = E M_0` of `conditioning.tex` is `optional_stopping` of [Probability/OptionalStopping](Lean/IPRADerivations/Probability/OptionalStopping.lean)), the distributions and the density realization are not yet derived. |

The inexact-iteration budget `eq:feedback-inexact` and the finite Neumann producer (residual identity and power bound) are derived in [Analysis/FeedbackInexact](Lean/IPRADerivations/Analysis/FeedbackInexact.lean). For the ODE section, `lem:ode-growth` at every node `j ≤ n` is `growth_le` of [Analysis/OdeGrowth](Lean/IPRADerivations/Analysis/OdeGrowth.lean) (the case `j = n` is `growth` of [Foundations/DiscreteGronwall](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/DiscreteGronwall.lean)); the arithmetic of `thm:ode-produced-refinement` once its recurrence `eq:ode-produced-recurrence` is given (Gronwall at the fine nodes and the geometric telescoping `eq:ode-produced-cauchy`) is `node_bound` and `geometric_cauchy` of [Foundations/OdeRefinement](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/OdeRefinement.lean) (checked against the statements). The signed Euler polygons and the residual identity `eq:ode-residual-identity`, the rounding schedule, the signed polynomial solver `thm:polysolver` with its residual bound, and the factor blueprint are not derived. In the cluster section, `prop:cluster-polynomial-observations` for nonnegative centers (power sums and elementary symmetric polynomials of matched lists) is derived in [Analysis/ClusterObservations](Lean/IPRADerivations/Analysis/ClusterObservations.lean); the Gaussian-rational form, `lem:coverage-hall`, `thm:bounded-matching`, component transport and stable locators are not derived. |

| [analysis/stirling_ipra](../docs/proofs/analysis/stirling_ipra/) | [Analysis/Products](Lean/IPRADerivations/Analysis/Products.lean), [Analysis/GapRemoval](Lean/IPRADerivations/Analysis/GapRemoval.lean), [Analysis/PowerIncrement](Lean/IPRADerivations/Analysis/PowerIncrement.lean), [Analysis/WallisGrid](Lean/IPRADerivations/Analysis/WallisGrid.lean), [Analysis/WallisRecurrence](Lean/IPRADerivations/Analysis/WallisRecurrence.lean), [Analysis/WallisFinite](Lean/IPRADerivations/Analysis/WallisFinite.lean); Section `sec:wallis` is checked in full: the grid identities `eq:sinc`, `eq:cinc`, `eq:powerr`, the recurrence `eq:Jrec`, `eq:evenerr`, `eq:odderr` and the finite Wallis inequality `thm:finitewallis` (also as a closed sentence) with its consequence `W_m ≤ 2`, together with the Wallis product identity and `lem:gap`; [Analysis/RobbinsSeries](Lean/IPRADerivations/Analysis/RobbinsSeries.lean) checks the rational series estimate `eq:dcompare` and the telescoper identities `eq:margins`, [Analysis/ExponentialSeries](Lean/IPRADerivations/Analysis/ExponentialSeries.lean) the partial-sum bounds `eq:etail` for `e`, and [Analysis/RobbinsTelescope](Lean/IPRADerivations/Analysis/RobbinsTelescope.lean) the telescoped Robbins inequality `eq:weaktel` for the series-named differences. [Analysis/WeierstrassProduct](Lean/IPRADerivations/Analysis/WeierstrassProduct.lean) gives the product inequality used before `eq:EB`. [Analysis/BinomialExponentialGap](Lean/IPRADerivations/Analysis/BinomialExponentialGap.lean) gives the comparison `eq:EB` term by term (each term of `B_m` is at most the term of `E_m`, and their difference is at most `(1/(2m)) · 1/(j − 2)!`), and [Analysis/StirlingElimination](Lean/IPRADerivations/Analysis/StirlingElimination.lean) the linear elimination steps `eq:terminal`, `eq:finitefinal`, `eq:Qfinite` and the scale inequalities of `eq:Herr`, with `eq:Fbound` and `eq:Qerr` as hypotheses. The logarithm and exponential names, the identification `eq:dseries`, `eq:Fbound`, `eq:Qerr` and `thm:main` remain. The summed form of `eq:EB` (`0 ≤ E_m − B_m ≤ 3/(2m)`, `2 ≤ B_m ≤ E_m ≤ 3`) is [Analysis/ExponentialBinomialSum](Lean/IPRADerivations/Analysis/ExponentialBinomialSum.lean). |

| [analytic-number-theory/prime_distribution_ipra](../docs/proofs/analytic-number-theory/prime_distribution_ipra/) | [Foundations/PrecisionArithmetic](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/PrecisionArithmetic.lean), [Foundations/LeastBoundedWitness](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/LeastBoundedWitness.lean); the elimination of a vanishing rational error and the bounded quantifier bits with least search are checked; the Dirichlet bound, the Brun-Titchmarsh and sieve inequalities, the Vaughan identity and the Gram certificate are not derived. |
| [analytic-number-theory/prime_reciprocal_ipra_2026-11-15](../docs/proofs/analytic-number-theory/prime_reciprocal_ipra_2026-11-15/) | [AnalyticNumberTheory/BinomialThree](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/BinomialThree.lean), [AnalyticNumberTheory/Harmonic](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/Harmonic.lean), [AnalyticNumberTheory/LocalFactor](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/LocalFactor.lean), [AnalyticNumberTheory/PowerCleared](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/PowerCleared.lean), [AnalyticNumberTheory/SumReindex](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/SumReindex.lean), [AnalyticNumberTheory/Smooth](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/Smooth.lean), [AnalyticNumberTheory/EulerDomination](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/EulerDomination.lean), [AnalyticNumberTheory/FactorVector](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/FactorVector.lean) |
| [analytic-number-theory/riemann_hypotheses_ipra_2026-11-20](../docs/proofs/analytic-number-theory/riemann_hypotheses_ipra_2026-11-20/) | [AnalyticNumberTheory/Riemann](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/Riemann/) (the packet Lean imported into the shared 50-module Riemann library). The three closed sentences RH, GRH, ERH (`Hypotheses`); in object iPRA ERH → GRH → RH by specialization (`Hierarchy`, `Hypotheses`) and each sentence ↔ the absence of a failing tuple (`RHCounterexamples`, `RiemannIPRA`); the standard reading and soundness at ℕ (`StandardReading`). The correspondence with critical-strip zeros is classical mathematics, not an iPRA derivation. |
| [analytic-number-theory/riemann_ipra_equivalences_2026-11-20](../docs/proofs/analytic-number-theory/riemann_ipra_equivalences_2026-11-20/) | [AnalyticNumberTheory/Riemann](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/Riemann/) (imported packet Lean). Object-iPRA biconditionals: first failure, failure counts, eventual/cofinal cleanliness and fixed PR sampling (`ObjectForms`), exact slack and slack certificates (`SlackForms`), `prog_grh_to_rh` (`SplitPrimes`), `dedekind_to_dirichlet`, `dirichlet_to_riemann` (`PrimeForms`), the Lagarias counterexample form (`Lagarias`). Standard-model and finite-algebra parts (18 discrete readings, the determinant identity `thm:matrix` as an integer-matrix identity, Farey/energy estimates, Abel identity) are Lean metatheory; `thm:rate` is a primitive-recursive witness transformation and is being derived: (2) ⇒ (1) ([AnalyticNumberTheory/RateAbel](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/RateAbel.lean) `recover`, with `u_N S_r(N)` cleared as a primitive recursion) and (1) ⇒ (2) ([AnalyticNumberTheory/RateShell](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/RateShell.lean) `w_all`, the shell majorants as an integer invariant) are done, and [AnalyticNumberTheory/RateIff](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/RateIff.lean) `rate_iff` proves (1) ↔ (2) in object iPRA for signed coefficient bodies, and [AnalyticNumberTheory/RateCauchy](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/RateCauchy.lean) / [AnalyticNumberTheory/RateCauchyIff](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/RateCauchyIff.lean) `rate3_iff` proves (1) ↔ (3) with the packet's explicit Cauchy modulus: `thm:rate` is complete. The analytic links (Mellin transforms, zero theory) are classical and not iPRA derivations. `thm:energy`: the recovery direction and its witness conversion are derived in object iPRA for every signed PR sequence `M = M⁺ − M⁻` given by two bodies: `d² ≤ 4J₂(d)` by the union bound ([AnalyticNumberTheory/EnergyJordan](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/EnergyJordan.lean) `jordan_quarter`), the cleared divisor inversion `Σ_{d≤N} μ(d) T'_d = N! M(N)` with the two signs of `T'_d = N! T_d` accumulated separately ([AnalyticNumberTheory/EnergyMobius](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/EnergyMobius.lean) `mobius_energy`), the cleared weighted Cauchy–Schwarz `(N!)² M(N)² ≤ 8 Q_N` with `Q_N = (N!)² E_N` ([Analysis/WeightedCauchySchwarz](Lean/IPRADerivations/Analysis/WeightedCauchySchwarz.lean), [AnalyticNumberTheory/EnergyRecovery](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/EnergyRecovery.lean) `energy_recovery`), and the conversion "energy bound `c` ⇒ `8^r c` for `M`" ([AnalyticNumberTheory/EnergyWitness](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/EnergyWitness.lean) `energy_witness`). Open for `thm:energy`: the forward dyadic-block estimate (a bound `c` for `M` gives `(512r)^r (c+1)` for the energy), and the link of the bodies `QN` to the packet's PR programs in `FareyEnergy.lean`. |
| [analytic-number-theory/riemann_ipra_formalization_2026-11-20](../docs/proofs/analytic-number-theory/riemann_ipra_formalization_2026-11-20/) | [AnalyticNumberTheory/Riemann](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/Riemann/) (imported packet Lean). Object-iPRA derivations: countdown and precision-budget changes of the terminal zero test (`CountdownTests`, `PrecisionBounds`), one-sided rotating sampling (`OneSided`), Cayley and Hardy–Toeplitz scheduled tests, Robin/Nicolas first-failure forms (`RobinNicolas`), the scheduled universal criteria (`UniversalCriteria`), the product certificate (`ProductCertificate`). Residue-space / Gram elimination, grid interpolation and strip reflection are Lean metatheory; the zero theorems behind the standard interpretations are classical. |
| [analytic-number-theory/riemann_ipra_prime_criteria_2026-11-20](../docs/proofs/analytic-number-theory/riemann_ipra_prime_criteria_2026-11-20/) | [AnalyticNumberTheory/CubeScan](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/CubeScan.lean); the object-iPRA results `thm:cubef` (one finite cube, witness-preserving) and the multiplicative certificate. The classical standard-model equivalences with RH/GRH/ERH (`thm:main`, `thm:split`, `thm:taylor`) are not iPRA derivations; `thm:symmetric-prefix` is derived in object iPRA (exponential, moment and product tests); `thm:powerdivisor`: its witness transfer is derived in object iPRA for arbitrary signed terms ([AnalyticNumberTheory/PowerBudget](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/PowerBudget.lean), [AnalyticNumberTheory/PowerTransfer](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/PowerTransfer.lean): `PowerTransfer.transfer`, the packet's `power_divisor_transfer`); both finite identities along perfect powers are derived for arbitrary coefficient bodies ([AnalyticNumberTheory/PowerDivisorPrefix](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/PowerDivisorPrefix.lean) `prefix_identity`, [AnalyticNumberTheory/PowerDivisorRow](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/PowerDivisorRow.lean), [AnalyticNumberTheory/PowerDivisorInversion](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/PowerDivisorInversion.lean) `inversion`); and the growth equivalence itself is derived: [AnalyticNumberTheory/PowerDivisorGrowth](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/PowerDivisorGrowth.lean) `growth_iff` proves `(∀k ∃c ∀N |A(N)|^{2(k+1)} ≤ cN^{k+2}) ↔ (same for B_h)` in object iPRA for every `h ≥ 2` and arbitrary signed coefficient bodies (so for the RH, cyclotomic and Dedekind rows once their coefficient bodies are supplied), with the packet's witness `C_r(c) = (c+1)(8r)^{2r}`. The single sentence uniform in `h` is derived too ([AnalyticNumberTheory/PowerDivisorUniform](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/PowerDivisorUniform.lean) `growth_uniform_iff`). For the residue-class rows, the residue transport ([AnalyticNumberTheory/PowerResidue](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/PowerResidue.lean)), the progression-row identity `B_{h;q,b}(N) = Σ_d [gcd(d^h,q)=1] A_{q,b_{d^h}}(⌊N/d^h⌋)` ([AnalyticNumberTheory/PowerTwistPrefix](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/PowerTwistPrefix.lean), [AnalyticNumberTheory/PowerResidueRows](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/PowerResidueRows.lean) `masked_B`), the forward transfer ([AnalyticNumberTheory/PowerResidueForward](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/PowerResidueForward.lean) `forward_res_core`) and an abstract Möbius inversion from a row identity ([AnalyticNumberTheory/PowerAbstractInversion](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/PowerAbstractInversion.lean) `inversion_g`) are derived, and so are the residue inversion over units ([AnalyticNumberTheory/PowerResidueInversion](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/PowerResidueInversion.lean) `inversion_res`), the backward transfer ([AnalyticNumberTheory/PowerResidueBackward](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/PowerResidueBackward.lean)) and the sentence-level equivalence ([AnalyticNumberTheory/PowerResidueGrowth](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/PowerResidueGrowth.lean) `growth_res_iff`: residue-uniform growth of `A_{q,b}` ↔ that of `B_{h;q,b}`, `q ≥ 2`, witness `C_r(c)`); the `q = 1` row is the ordinary `growth_iff`. Not yet derived: the identification of the packet's compiled coefficient programs with these bodies., [AnalyticNumberTheory/SymmetricPrefix](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/SymmetricPrefix.lean), [AnalyticNumberTheory/SymmetricMoment](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/SymmetricMoment.lean), [AnalyticNumberTheory/SymmetricProduct](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/SymmetricProduct.lean), [AnalyticNumberTheory/Riemann](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/Riemann/) (imported packet Lean, incl. `CubeTests`, `SymmetricPrefixes`, `TaylorPackets`, `LiouvilleCriterion`) |
| [analytic-number-theory/robin_ipra_research_2026-11-19](../docs/proofs/analytic-number-theory/robin_ipra_research_2026-11-19/) | [AnalyticNumberTheory/RobinLogic](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/RobinLogic.lean); the logical layer (`prop:logic`, `thm:assembly`, the packet Lean shell) in object iPRA. [AnalyticNumberTheory/RobinGamma](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/RobinGamma.lean) derives the finite telescoping core of `lem:gamma` in object iPRA: with `log(1+1/m)` named by the cleared truncations `S_K`, the increments `δ_K(m)` of `a_m = H_m − log m − 1/(2m)` dominate the explicit truncation tail (`q_inv`), satisfy `δ_K(m) ≤ 1/(12m²) − 1/(12(m+1)²)` for `K ≥ 2` (`p_inv`), and telescope to `Σ_{m₀≤i<M} δ_K(i) ≤ 1/(12m₀²) − 1/(12M²)` (`gamma_tail`); the identification of the limit name with the analytic Euler constant is external. The multiplicativity of `σ` used by the lcm bridge and `thm:three` is derived ([AnalyticNumberTheory/DivisorFactor](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/DivisorFactor.lean) `divisor_factor`: `Σ_{d∣mN} g(d) = Σ_{a∣m}Σ_{b∣N} g(ab)` for coprime `m, N`, and [AnalyticNumberTheory/SigmaMult](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/SigmaMult.lean) `sigma_mul`). The prime-power formula `(p−1)σ(p^a) + 1 = p^{a+1}` ([AnalyticNumberTheory/SigmaPrimePower](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/SigmaPrimePower.lean)) and the integer half of `thm:three`, `4σ(n) < 15n` for every `n` with at most three distinct prime factors ([AnalyticNumberTheory/RobinThree](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/RobinThree.lean) `three_core`), are derived, as is the general product formula `σ(n) Π_{p∣n}(p−1) = Π_{p∣n}(p^{v_p(n)+1}−1)` behind both the `thm:three` estimate and the lcm bridge's exact identity ([AnalyticNumberTheory/SigmaProduct](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/SigmaProduct.lean) `sigma_product`). The integer half of the lcm bridge's rough Chebyshev bound is derived: `2^{m²−1} ≤ (m²+1)(m²)^m ∏_{p≤m²} p` ([AnalyticNumberTheory/PrimorialLower](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/PrimorialLower.lean) `primorial_lower`, from `lcm_primorial` and `two_pow_lcm`). The explicit interval arithmetic is derived on rational names: nested logarithm names for `eq:logtail` with the uniform tail `eq:uniform-log-tail` ([AnalyticNumberTheory/RobinLogName](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/RobinLogName.lean)), `LogI(q, k)` with binary range reduction, consistent across precisions and monotone in `q` ([AnalyticNumberTheory/RobinLogInterval](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/RobinLogInterval.lean)), the nested exponential names of `eq:exptail` ([AnalyticNumberTheory/RobinExpName](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/RobinExpName.lean)), and the Euler-constant enclosure `γ_lo(t, K) ≤ γ_hi(t, K')` inside `[1/2, 7/12]`, with `γ_lo ≥ 4/7` for `t ≥ 3` ([AnalyticNumberTheory/RobinGammaInterval](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/RobinGammaInterval.lean), [AnalyticNumberTheory/RobinGammaUpper](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/RobinGammaUpper.lean)). `thm:three` is complete in the certificate form `eq:pos`: the interval program `L(n, k)` (double logarithm at precision `r = k + ℓ(n) + 16`, times `E_{K_e}(γ_lo)`) and `Pos(n, k)`, with `B(n) ≥ B(5041) > 15/4` derived on the names by monotonicity and nesting down to three closed cross-multiplications, give `Pos(p^a q^b r^c, k)` for all `k` whenever `p^a q^b r^c ≥ 5041` ([AnalyticNumberTheory/RobinThreePos](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/RobinThreePos.lean) `three_pos`). The upper end `U(n, k)`, `Neg` (`eq:neg`) and the incompatibility of `Pos(n, k)` with `Neg(n, k')` at any two precisions are derived ([AnalyticNumberTheory/RobinUpperEnd](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/RobinUpperEnd.lean) `lu`, `pos_neg`), as are the width `≤ 2^{-k}` of `LogI(q, k)` ([AnalyticNumberTheory/RobinLogWidth](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/RobinLogWidth.lean) `logI_width`) and the elementary inequalities `1 − 1/q ≤ log q ≤ q − 1` on the names ([AnalyticNumberTheory/RobinLogDiff](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/RobinLogDiff.lean)). Not derived: the width bounds `≤ 2^{-k}` of the composed (double-logarithm, `γ`, `e^γ`, product) intervals; the packet's schedule is followed except that `γ` telescopes from `a_1 = 1/2` (the construction of `lem:gamma`'s proof) and the `[1/2, 7/12]`, `[1, 3]` intersections are omitted. The rough Chebyshev lemma of the lcm bridge is derived on the logarithm names: `ϑ(m²) ≥ m²/4` for every `m ≥ 16` ([AnalyticNumberTheory/LcmBridgeTheta](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/LcmBridgeTheta.lean), [AnalyticNumberTheory/LcmBridgeSide](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/LcmBridgeSide.lean) `theta_m16`). The finite schedule of the eta checker (Taylor cutoff and error budgets) is derived in [AnalyticNumberTheory/EtaSchedule](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/EtaSchedule.lean); its analytic adequacy (argument principle, completeness) is the packet's own open obligation. The `T` loss is derived in the product form in which it enters the bridge: `∏_{p≤m²}(1 − p^{−a_p−1}) ≥ 1 − 2/m` ([AnalyticNumberTheory/LcmBridgeLoss](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/LcmBridgeLoss.lean) `loss_sum`, `prod_lower`), so the transfer needs no log-name additivity. The finite counterexample transfer is derived in product form: for `m ≥ 2` and `L = lcm(1, …, m²)`, the name-level hypothesis `∏_{p∣L}(1 − 1/p)·[e^γ log log L]_hi < 1 − 2/m` gives `NegR(L, k)` ([AnalyticNumberTheory/LcmBridgeTransfer](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/LcmBridgeTransfer.lean) `transfer`, with the exact identity of [AnalyticNumberTheory/LcmBridgeExact](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/LcmBridgeExact.lean) and `SigmaProduct.sigma_product`). The comparisons of logarithm names of different arguments are derived: Lipschitz bounds `2(z′ − z)(1 − z^{2K})/(1 − z²) ≤ S_K(z′) − S_K(z) ≤ 2(z′ − z)/(1 − z′²)` for the artanh truncations ([AnalyticNumberTheory/RobinLipName](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/RobinLipName.lean); the upper names `U_K` do not satisfy the upper bound, their slope exceeds `2/(1 − z²)`), the two-sided bounds `(x′ − x)/x′ − (x′ − x)/(x 9^K) ≤ lo(x′, K) − lo(x, K) ≤ (x′ − x)/x` for `x ≤ x′ ≤ 2x` across binades ([AnalyticNumberTheory/RobinLipArg](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/RobinLipArg.lean), [AnalyticNumberTheory/RobinLipLog](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/RobinLipLog.lean)), and the finitary addition theorem `|lo(xc, K) − lo(x, K) − lo(c, K)| ≤ (c − 1)/9^K` for all rationals `x, c ≥ 1`, by a finite Riemann-sum comparison along `x t_i`, `t_i` with `N = E + 1` steps ([AnalyticNumberTheory/RobinLipSum](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/RobinLipSum.lean) `lo_sub_up`, `lo_sub_low`), with `lo(q) ≤ q − 1`, `lo(4) ≤ 3/2` and `lo(xP^j) ≤ lo(x) + j lo(P) + j(P−1)/9^K` ([AnalyticNumberTheory/RobinLipBound](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/RobinLipBound.lean)). The loss `D(m²) ≤ 8/m` is derived in multiplicative form on the names: for `m ≥ 16`, `UH ≤ (1 + 8/m)·H_θ`, where `UH` is the double-logarithm upper name of the Robin upper end `U(L, k)` and `H_θ = hi(log Θ, K_h)` with `Θ = hi(log ∏_{p≤m²} p, K_θ)` an upper name of `ϑ(m²)` at any precisions `K_θ ≥ 2`, `K_h` ([AnalyticNumberTheory/LcmBridgeDLoss](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/LcmBridgeDLoss.lean) `dloss`, through `ψ̂ ≤ Θ + m·lo(log m²) + 1` of [AnalyticNumberTheory/LcmBridgeDPsi](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/LcmBridgeDPsi.lean) `psi_le`, i.e. `ψ ≤ ϑ + m log P` on names by the addition theorem). The transfer is thereby restated through `ϑ(m²)`: `∏_{p∣L}(1 − 1/p)·[e^γ]_hi·H_θ·(1 + 8/m) < 1 − 2/m` gives `NegR(L, k)` (`transfer_theta`), and so does the packet form `F_hi(m²) = ∏(1 − 1/p)·[e^γ]_hi·H_θ < 1 − 12/m` (`transfer_theta12`; it gives `−log F(m²) > 12/m`). The composed double-logarithm width is derived on the branch `log n ≥ 1`: `hi(log x′, K′) − lo(log x, K′) ≤ (x′ − x)/x + 2^{−k}` for an enclosure `[x, x′] ⊆ [1, 2x]` ([AnalyticNumberTheory/RobinLipWidth](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/RobinLipWidth.lean) `comp_width`). Open: the composed `2^{−k}` widths of `report.tex` §"Explicit interval arithmetic" beyond that lemma — the instantiation at `L(n, k)`/`U(n, k)` (the precisions `K₂ = Kp(A₁, B₁, r)`, `K₃ = Kp(C₁, D₁, r)` differ, so it also needs the nesting between them), the branch `log n < 1` (logarithm of the reciprocal), the width of the `γ` enclosure (`γ_hi(t, K) − γ_lo(t, K)` from `RobinGamma.gamma_tail` and the log widths), the exponential Lipschitz bound `T_K(u) − E_K(l) ≤ 3(u − l) + 2/(K+1)!` for the `e^γ` width, and the four-corner product `(n + 12)2^{−r} ≤ 2^{−k}`; none of these is needed for the soundness of `Pos`/`Neg` (`lu`, `pos_neg`). Also open: the analytic adequacy of the eta checker. |

| [foundations/cifer_ipra_framework_2026-11-15](../docs/proofs/foundations/cifer_ipra_framework_2026-11-15/) | [Foundations/GapRecovery](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/GapRecovery.lean). The bisection invariant (sign bracket and width at every stage, for an abstract test and sign predicates) is derived in [Foundations/BisectionInvariant](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/BisectionInvariant.lean); the cubic chart `y³ + y = x` for a nonnegative rational input is instantiated in [Foundations/CubicBisection](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/CubicBisection.lean) (bracket at every stage, nestedness and the width fuel); the signed input, the regular midpoint name and the represented equation are not derived. The width at the fuel stage and the nestedness of all later brackets (`thm:cubic-enclosure`) and the regularity of the midpoint name are derived in [Foundations/CubicEnclosure](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/CubicEnclosure.lean). The residual estimate of the represented equation at the endpoints of the bracket is `endpoint_residual` of [Foundations/CubicEnclosure](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/CubicEnclosure.lean). Statements of this packet derived under other packets (each checked against the statement): bounded enumeration (`thm:bounded-enumeration`: sound, complete, strictly increasing) and the height/list conversions (`thm:height-to-list`, `thm:list-to-height`) are `listed`, `entry`, `sel_strict`, `complete_of_bound` and `bound_of_list` of [Foundations/BoundedEnumeration](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/BoundedEnumeration.lean); stable-observer elimination and diameter elimination (`thm:stable-observer`, `thm:diameter-elimination`) are `exact_erasure` and `metric_erasure` of [Foundations/ObserverErasure](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/ObserverErasure.lean); the canonical finite path (`cor:least-finite-path`) is the least-successor path of [Foundations/LeastViablePath](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/LeastViablePath.lean). Further statements derived under other modules (each checked against the statement, 19 November 2026): `thm:residual-compilation` is `frontier_sufficient` of [Foundations/ReachableFrontier](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/ReachableFrontier.lean) with `contract_iff_sequent` of [Foundations/OpenContracts](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/OpenContracts.lean); `cor:boundary-discharge` is `residual_discharge` and `cut_all`; "Irrelevant boundaries are absent" is `front_only_reachable`; `thm:producer-verifier` is `instantiate_into` and `cut_all` of [Foundations/StagedGraph](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/StagedGraph.lean); `thm:qf-reflection` is `bounded_reflection`/`bit_cases` of [Foundations/BoundedSyntax](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/BoundedSyntax.lean) (falsity as a false atom); `thm:least-demand` is the demand closure of [Foundations/FiniteStabilization](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/FiniteStabilization.lean) and [Foundations/DemandClosure](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/DemandClosure.lean); `thm:trace-compilation` is `trace_at`/`trace` of [Foundations/TraceReflection](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/TraceReflection.lean); `thm:proof-fold` is `all_derivable` of [Foundations/ProofSchedule](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/ProofSchedule.lean); "Auxiliary exhaustion" is `exhaustive_complete`/`exhaustive_sound` of [Foundations/ExhaustiveWitness](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/ExhaustiveWitness.lean). Partly derived: `thm:cubic-inverse` for nonnegative rational input (existence from [Foundations/CubicEnclosure](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/CubicEnclosure.lean), uniqueness from the quantitative inverse Lipschitz bound `approx_unique` of [Foundations/CubicInverse](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/CubicInverse.lean); the assembled represented-root statement and negative inputs remain); `thm:shrinking-coherence` (the final estimate `d(ω(a_p), ω(a_q)) ≤ τ(p) + δ(r) + τ(q)` is `three_term` of [Foundations/TelescopingNames](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/TelescopingNames.lean); the common-horizon extension of the selected candidates is not composed); the derivation-level content of "Monotonic progress" (replacing a boundary assumption by a derived node with residual set `S`: the downstream derivations are read from the new context) is simultaneous cut, `Prf.trans`, which is the `supply` case of [Foundations/ProofPlan](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/ProofPlan.lean); the residual-set bookkeeping is metalevel. In `lem:reachability`, reachability as the least parent-closed set is `Reach`, `reach_parent`, `reach_min` of [Foundations/ReachableFrontier](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/ReachableFrontier.lean); the sweep algorithm and the bound on the number of sweeps are not derived. |
| [foundations/darc_ipra_framework_2026-11-16](../docs/proofs/foundations/darc_ipra_framework_2026-11-16/) | [Foundations/FiniteStabilization](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/FiniteStabilization.lean); the abstract ranked-step core of finite stabilization and the one-dimensional capped closure under inflation/monotonicity/cap contracts are checked; the vector rank lemma, viability, observational descent and the compilation theorems retain the scope stated below; the open contract rules are now checked. Statements of this packet already derived under other packets: the Booleanization theorem (`thm:bounded-reflection`) is the bounded-formula compiler of [Foundations/BoundedFormulas](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/BoundedFormulas.lean) with [Foundations/BooleanizationCorollaries](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/BooleanizationCorollaries.lean); trace reflection (`thm:trace-reflection`, the time-independent case) is [Foundations/TraceReflection](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/TraceReflection.lean); the viability/path theorem (`thm:finite-viability`) is [Foundations/BackwardViability](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/BackwardViability.lean), [Foundations/ViableChain](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/ViableChain.lean) and [Foundations/ChainViability](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/ChainViability.lean) for chains presented by terms, without the bounded existential over coded paths. The structural lemmas (simultaneous cut, weakening, substitution, existential sealing, the induction interface) are checked in [Foundations/StructuralRules](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/StructuralRules.lean). The contract/sequent equivalence is `contract_iff_sequent` of [Foundations/OpenContracts](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/OpenContracts.lean). The one-dimensional case of the least capped closure (fixed point at time `C`, above the start, least among prefixed points) is `capped_closure` and `capped_closure_least` of [Foundations/FiniteStabilization](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/FiniteStabilization.lean). The vector rank lemma (componentwise order gives rank order; equal coordinates or strictly larger rank) is checked in [Foundations/VectorRank](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/VectorRank.lean); the assembly with a concrete coding of vectors is not derived. Finite stabilization for vector states (stalled or rank at least the clock; stable and fixed at time `H` under a rank bound) is checked in [Foundations/VectorStabilization](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/VectorStabilization.lean) for any coding that preserves the length, is inflated coordinatewise by the update and is extensional. Acyclic graph compilation (`thm:acyclic-compile`) is `all_derivable` of [Foundations/ProofSchedule](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/ProofSchedule.lean) for local rules listed in topological order. Full discharge and incremental producer discharge are `full_discharge` and `discharge_some` of [Foundations/OpenContracts](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/OpenContracts.lean). Statements already derived elsewhere: named export (`thm:named-export`) is `Search.search_found` of Core/Search; local certificate erasure is `existential_sealing` of Foundations/StructuralRules; residual exactness relative to summaries is, for a one-dimensional demand state, `capped_closure_least` of Foundations/FiniteStabilization. Compositional soundness, dead-node elimination, loop slicing, fixed-plan specialization and the soundness theorem of the plan compiler are not derived. Statements of this packet derived under other packets (each checked against the statement): finite universal and existential reflection (`lem:all-reflect`, `lem:any-reflect`, for a bit read as true when nonzero) are `all_elim`, `all_intro`, `all_reflect`, `ex_intro`, `ex_elim` of [Foundations/BoundedReflection](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/BoundedReflection.lean); the canonical finite path (`thm:least-path`) is `path_inv`, `path_local`, `path_edge` of [Foundations/LeastViablePath](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/LeastViablePath.lean); stable observer elimination and local certificate erasure (`thm:observer-elimination`, `thm:local-cert-erasure`) are, for a bounded-search certificate, `exact_erasure` and `sealed_consumer` of [Foundations/ObserverErasure](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/ObserverErasure.lean) (for an arbitrary existential they are existential elimination itself). Partly derived under other modules (checked against the statements, 19 November 2026): the one-dimensional case of `cor:relative-minimality` (`capped_closure_least` of [Foundations/FiniteStabilization](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/FiniteStabilization.lean)) and the metric case of `thm:observational-descent` (`telescope` of [Foundations/TelescopingNames](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/TelescopingNames.lean) along the path of [Foundations/LeastViablePath](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/LeastViablePath.lean)); partly derived: the closure argument of `prop:dead-node` (every dependency of a retained node is retained, so the nodes reachable from the query derive it and the others can be dropped) is `reach_parent`, `reach_min` and `frontier_sufficient` of [Foundations/ReachableFrontier](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/ReachableFrontier.lean); the demand fixed point `d*` with `F(d*) ≤ d*` is not formalized.; partly derived: for `thm:darc-soundness`, the derivation-level assembly (structural compilation of a plan into an object derivation by stored derivations, substitution, weakening, simultaneous cut, sealing and implication introduction) is `compile` of [Foundations/ProofPlan](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/ProofPlan.lean); loop and trace nodes are [Foundations/TraceComposition](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/TraceComposition.lean) and [Foundations/TraceClosure](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/TraceClosure.lean), acyclic graphs [Foundations/ForwardValidity](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/ForwardValidity.lean); the packet-specific graph syntax, its well-formedness checks and the code-level compiler are not formalized. `thm:residual-exactness` (every demand state above the query satisfying the summaries lies above `d*`) is the leastness clause of `demand_closure` of [Foundations/DemandClosure](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/DemandClosure.lean) (`iterate_le_prefixed_at`: the query is folded into the transfer, so such a state is a prefixed point), with the capped form `capped_closure_least` of [Foundations/FiniteStabilization](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/FiniteStabilization.lean). "DARC compilation, informal" is partly derived as `thm:darc-soundness` above (`compile`, `demand_closure`, `trace`, `discharge_some` of [Foundations/OpenContracts](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/OpenContracts.lean)); its plan syntax and termination are metalevel. `thm:fixed-specialization` ("Fixed-plan specialization"): item 4 (a Lean term from the obligations) is `compile` of [Foundations/ProofPlan](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/ProofPlan.lean); items 1 to 3 are code generation at the metalevel. `prop:loop-slicing` ("Loop slicing"): trace reflection applies to any sliced step (`trace` of [Foundations/TraceReflection](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/TraceReflection.lean)); the correctness of slicing and projection is not formalized. |

`prop:det-cert-reflection` is `cert_accept` and `cert_run` of [Foundations/TraceCertificate](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/TraceCertificate.lean); `prop:rel-cert` is `path_local` and `path_post` of [Foundations/RelationalCertificate](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/RelationalCertificate.lean) (the extracted path by bounded descent on the resource); the contraction trace and uniqueness of `thm:regular-branch` are `contraction_iter` and `contraction_unique`, with the step count `steps_suffice` (`N = a M` steps give `q^N ≤ 1/(1 + M)`), of [Foundations/ContractionTrace](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/ContractionTrace.lean); partly derived: `cor:unordered-root` is `exact_erasure` of [Foundations/ObserverErasure](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/ObserverErasure.lean) with the observer the symmetric output (the least accepted labeling has the output of every admissible labeling), given the stability of the output under relabelings; the algebraic symmetry proof that supplies this stability is not formalized. `thm:cluster-refine` ("Cluster refinement as a relational producer") is not derived. |
| [foundations/ipra_framework](../docs/proofs/foundations/ipra_framework/) | [DiophantineNumberTheory/Siegel](Lean/IPRADerivations/DiophantineNumberTheory/Siegel.lean); [Foundations/RiseAlternative](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/RiseAlternative.lean) derives the finite rise alternative `lem:rise`; [Analysis/PowerGap](Lean/IPRADerivations/Analysis/PowerGap.lean) has the integer power gap `dio:gap` for naturals `u < v` (`strict_power_gap`) and the finite loss estimate `dio:loss` (`power_gap` with `U = B`); the polynomial lower bound and root bound `dio:polynomialbounds` are `dominance` and `no_large_root` of [Foundations/SplitBranches](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/SplitBranches.lean); rational separation (`lem:rational-separation`) is the gap lemma of Analysis/GapRemoval with a different explicit precision. [Foundations/FiniteReplay](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/FiniteReplay.lean) derives finite replay (`lem:replay`), [Foundations/PrecisionGate](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/PrecisionGate.lean) the precision budget `core:gate` (the multiplication gate and the error recurrence), and [Foundations/WindowStability](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/WindowStability.lean) the two selection arguments of the window theorems (`thm:rational-window`: equal floors confine the window; `thm:real-window`: the minimal shifted difference). The bounded-loop rule (`logic:boundedloop`) is `guarded_trace` of Foundations/GuardedTrace and Booleanization (`logic:booleanization`) is Foundations/BoundedFormulas. The counting core of the empty-window theorem (`thm:empty-window`) is [Foundations/EmptyWindow](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/EmptyWindow.lean). The nonrise index of the rational window is `floor_nonrise` of [Foundations/RationalWindow](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/RationalWindow.lean). The estimate at the selected endpoints of the represented window is `selected_increase` of [Foundations/RealWindow](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/RealWindow.lean). The candidate starts, the choice of `K` and the final margins of the two window theorems, the analytic producers and the certificate theorems `dio:height` and `dio:completion` of the Diophantine section are not derived; `dio:family` (the integer points of `y^m = x^{mr} + x + 1`, uniformly in `m ≥ 3`, `r ≥ 1`) is [DiophantineNumberTheory/SuperellipticFamily](Lean/IPRADerivations/DiophantineNumberTheory/SuperellipticFamily.lean), without `dio:height`, by the power gap directly. A reusable induction principle with a shifting parameter is [Foundations/ShiftedInduction](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/ShiftedInduction.lean). Statements derived under other modules (each checked against the statement, 19 November 2026): `logic:noeval` is `no_universal_primitive_recursive_evaluator` of [Foundations/UniversalEvaluatorObstruction](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/UniversalEvaluatorObstruction.lean) and `no_universal_evaluator` of [Foundations/EmbeddingBasics](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/EmbeddingBasics.lean). Partly derived: the first clause of `ana:selection` (`approximate_witness`, `argmin_minimal` of [Foundations/ApproximateWitness](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/ApproximateWitness.lean)); `thm:integral` in dimension one (the grouping of the fine samples by coarse cells and the finite weighted-sum bound `the distance of S_l and S_k is at most e`, `grid_upper` and `grid_lower` of [Analysis/GridIntegral](Lean/IPRADerivations/Analysis/GridIntegral.lean)); `thm:observation-composition` is `compose_obs` of [Foundations/ObservationComposition](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/ObservationComposition.lean), with validity and guarantees as bits. In `ana:series`, item 1 (`eq:ana:iterate`: the iterates stay in the ball and `‖Z_m − Z_n‖ ≤ ρ 2^{−n}`) is `cauchy_iterates` of [Foundations/ContractionIterates](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/ContractionIterates.lean), from the two conclusions of `ana:contraction` taken as rules; the coefficient names of item 2 remain. `logic:compilation` ("Finite contract compilation") is partly derived: `compile` of [Foundations/ProofPlan](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/ProofPlan.lean), `guarded_loop` of [Foundations/GuardedTrace](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/GuardedTrace.lean) and `branch_preserved` of [Foundations/TraceClosure](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/TraceClosure.lean); the program language is metalevel. Not derived: `ana:producer` (the certificate producer on a simple-root chart) and `dio:denominators` (bounded denominator transport, which needs `dio:height`). |
| [foundations/ipra_framework_2026-11-15](../docs/proofs/foundations/ipra_framework_2026-11-15/) | [Foundations/PrecisionArithmetic](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/PrecisionArithmetic.lean), [Foundations/LeastBoundedWitness](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/LeastBoundedWitness.lean); the cleared numerator inequality cores for finite-tail replacement and effective inversion under apartness, finite-certificate composition and the specification of bounded existential search are checked. Represented tail equality, the actual inverse-name precision schedule and its representation adapter remain; finite descent with explicit range is the theorem `terminal` of [Foundations/BoundedDescent](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/BoundedDescent.lean) with the terminal test "height zero", under that module's extra clause that the step fixes terminal states (without it the iterate after the height first reaches zero is not controlled); the search from a supplied height bound is `witness_of_bound` and `decide_of_bound` of [Foundations/LeastBoundedWitness](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/LeastBoundedWitness.lean) (with the complete list in Foundations/BoundedEnumeration); for the algebra of dyadic Cauchy names, the synchronized sum estimate is `sum_modulus` of Foundations/PrecisionArithmetic and the monotone normalization of a modulus is [Foundations/ModulusNormalization](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/ModulusNormalization.lean); the assembly of the sum name with its representation predicate is not derived. The no-uniform-zero-decision theorem is a statement about computable procedures, not an iPRA derivation. "Finite descent with explicit range" is false as stated (the height can reach 0 before step `h(x)`, after which the step is unconstrained); the repaired lemma, with the guarded step `T'(x) = x` at height 0, is `explicit_range` of [Foundations/GuardedDescent](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/GuardedDescent.lean), and `explicit_range_fix` gives the unguarded iterate when `T` fixes valid height-0 states. In "Safe alternatives", the core of clause (ii) (the sample lower bound and the reciprocal error) is `apart_lower`/`inverse_error` of [Foundations/PrecisionArithmetic](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/PrecisionArithmetic.lean). "No uniform zero-decision from Cauchy names" is a statement about computable procedures, not an iPRA derivation. |

The assembled cubic finite-use certificate (`thm:cubic`) is not derived; the closure lemma (fixed products, sums, bounded lists, tables and folds are primitive recursive, with quantifier-free graphs) is a property of the term language itself: every fixed construction is a `PR` term built by composition and primitive recursion (`ConciseIPRA/PR`), with the bounded sums, products and folds `sumF`, `prodF`, `rec2` and the pairing `pairT` of `Core`; it needs no separate iPRA derivation. "Closure of fixed bounded data operations" is a closure property of the primitive-recursive class, and "Untrusted generation, trusted checking" describes the Lean and JSON tooling; both are metalevel. |

Deterministic contract composition (`thm:det-comp`) is `compose` of Foundations/OpenContracts (substitution followed by simultaneous cut). Residual monotonicity in the sink set (`lem:residual-monotonicity`, first part) is `reach_mono` and `front_union` of Foundations/ReachableFrontier, and residual irrelevance (`lem:residual-irrelevance`) is `front_agree` of [Foundations/QueryCompilation](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/QueryCompilation.lean) with `weakening` of Foundations/StructuralRules. Statements derived under other modules (each checked against the statement, 19 November 2026): the clauses of `thm:finite-rel-main` are `suffix_iff` of [Foundations/CodedViableSuffix](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/CodedViableSuffix.lean), `returned_admissible` of [Foundations/ViableChainSelection](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/ViableChainSelection.lean), `exact_erasure`/`metric_erasure` of [Foundations/ObserverErasure](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/ObserverErasure.lean) and `regular_of_budget` of [Foundations/TelescopingNames](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/TelescopingNames.lean); `cor:raw-demand-adequacy` (one coordinate) is `raw_adequate` and `raw_least` of [Foundations/RawDemand](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/RawDemand.lean). "Structural adequacy" is a metalevel translation from sources to graphs. `thm:residual-main` ("Residualized kernel compilation") is partly derived: the residual cut `residual_cut` of [Foundations/StructuralRules](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/StructuralRules.lean), `residual_discharge` of [Foundations/OpenContracts](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/OpenContracts.lean) and `frontier_sufficient` of [Foundations/ReachableFrontier](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/ReachableFrontier.lean); the graph compiler remains. |

The vector rank lemma (componentwise order gives rank order; equal coordinates or strictly larger rank) is checked in [Foundations/VectorRank](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/VectorRank.lean); the assembly with a concrete coding of vectors is not derived. Finite stabilization for vector states (stalled or rank at least the clock; stable and fixed at time `H` under a rank bound) is checked in [Foundations/VectorStabilization](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/VectorStabilization.lean) for any coding that preserves the length, is inflated coordinatewise by the update and is extensional. Finite dependent choice by least viability (`thm:finite-choice`) is derived in [Foundations/LeastViablePath](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/LeastViablePath.lean) (least viable start by bounded search, then the least passing successor at every level); the label-indexed invariant of the one-loop normal form (`thm:one-loop`) with its case split on the program counter is [Foundations/LabelledInvariant](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/LabelledInvariant.lean), while the program transformation itself is not formalized. [Foundations/LexFuelMonotone](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/LexFuelMonotone.lean) proves fuel monotonicity for every fixed external `d`. Original numerical-degree elimination, fiber multiplicity, Bezout and concrete elimination/compiler frontends remain separate. Additional predetermined-demand coherence derivations are in [Foundations/DemandRecursion](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/DemandRecursion.lean). [Foundations/LexPotential](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/LexPotential.lean) and [Foundations/LexChain](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/LexChain.lean) derive `thm:controlled-lex` for every fixed dimension. [Foundations/AutomaticLexFuel](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/AutomaticLexFuel.lean) derives `thm:automatic-lex-fuel`. [Foundations/AutomaticLexFuelExample](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/AutomaticLexFuelExample.lean) is an instance of it with every premise discharged. [Foundations/TermCompiler](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/TermCompiler.lean) supplies term-to-symbol compiler correctness; [Foundations/ExhaustiveWitness](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/ExhaustiveWitness.lean) supplies bounded-existential membership transfer and first-success uniqueness; actual stored duplicate-free unions remain separate. [Foundations/AuxiliaryEnvelope](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/AuxiliaryEnvelope.lean) supplies sum-of-supplied-lengths arithmetic and class counting under a stronger global classifier-range premise. The original finite-list-only coverage, first-cover assignment and auxiliary-envelope producer remain separate. [Foundations/EliminationDegree](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/EliminationDegree.lean) derives the degree count of `lem:elim-degree` for one term of the determinant expansion (`Σ_i β_i = e d`) and sums over a permuted range. [Foundations/PowerBezout](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/PowerBezout.lean) derives the explicit Bézout witnesses for two coprime powers used in `thm:elim-bezout`. The lemma "Guarded finite execution" (`lem:execution`) is `loop_at` of [Foundations/GuardedLoop](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/GuardedLoop.lean), with the Booleanized bounded invariant as the quantifier-free invariant, the step obligation guarded by `t < B`, and the fixed context carried as the guard formula. Partly derived under other modules (checked against the statements, 19 November 2026): the two-level scan of `thm:elim-complete` (`inter_sound`, `inter_complete`, `inter_height` of [Foundations/IntersectionEnumeration](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/IntersectionEnumeration.lean), `scan_nodup` of [Foundations/RationalRootScan](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/RationalRootScan.lean), `elim_root` of [Foundations/EliminationRoot](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/EliminationRoot.lean); the shear, the coefficient tests and the length bound are not assembled) and the supplied-cover part of `cor:elim-global-cover` (`global_cover`, `global_height`); `prop:composition` is `compose` of [Foundations/OperationComposition](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/OperationComposition.lean) (contracts as rules on proofs; the prefix maximum of several demands is `ModulusNormalization.modulus_le`). "The uniform diagonal is not primitive recursive" is a metatheorem about the primitive-recursive class. |

"Dependency exactness" is `front_only_reachable`/`front_of_reachable` of [Foundations/ReachableFrontier](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/ReachableFrontier.lean); the forward construction of "Least viable path" is [Foundations/LeastViablePath](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/LeastViablePath.lean) with `start_viable` of [Foundations/ChainViability](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/ChainViability.lean). `thm:scoped-realization`, `cor:conditional` and `thm:dependent-sealing` are `certificate_sealing`, `conditional_boundary` and `dependent_sealing` of [Foundations/SealingVariants](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/SealingVariants.lean); `thm:observed-relation` (existence and uniqueness of the observed relation) is `out_exists` and `out_unique` of [Foundations/ObservedRelation](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/ObservedRelation.lean); `thm:plan-soundness` is `compile` of [Foundations/ProofPlan](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/ProofPlan.lean), over the paper's indexed plan language `Plan` (the graph frontend passes are not formalized). `thm:scope-normal-form` ("Scope-minimal normal form") is partly derived: item (iv) is `front_only_reachable` of [Foundations/ReachableFrontier](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/ReachableFrontier.lean) and sealing is the `sealed` plan of [Foundations/ProofPlan](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/ProofPlan.lean); the scope-minimization transformation remains. "No dangling certificate" is derived for the sealed case by typing (the conclusion is at the outer arity, `compile` of [Foundations/ProofPlan](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/ProofPlan.lean)); the named and relational cases are not modelled. |
| [foundations/scoped_certificate_realization_ipra_2026-11-16](../docs/proofs/foundations/scoped_certificate_realization_ipra_2026-11-16/) | [Foundations/LeastBoundedWitness](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/LeastBoundedWitness.lean), [Foundations/GapStabilization](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/GapStabilization.lean); the least accepted certificate and the label gap-stabilization lemma are checked; the remaining theorems are not derived. Statements derived under other modules (each checked against the statement, 19 November 2026): `thm:seal` and `thm:dependent-seal` are `existential_sealing` of [Foundations/StructuralRules](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/StructuralRules.lean) (once and twice); "Naturality" is its `specialization`; "Bounded-existential reflection" is `exists_of_lt`/`lt_of_exists` of [Foundations/LeastBoundedWitness](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/LeastBoundedWitness.lean); `thm:strategy-sound` is `strategy_sound`/`bounded_strategy` of [Foundations/StrategyRealization](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/StrategyRealization.lean). Partly derived: the rank-increase stabilization of "Bounded cyclic demand closure" ([Foundations/FiniteStabilization](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/FiniteStabilization.lean), [Foundations/VectorRank](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/VectorRank.lean)).; partly derived: for `thm:compiler` (SCR compiler soundness), the derivation-level assembly (structural compilation of a plan into an object derivation by stored derivations, substitution, weakening, simultaneous cut, sealing and implication introduction) is `compile` of [Foundations/ProofPlan](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/ProofPlan.lean); loop and trace nodes are [Foundations/TraceComposition](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/TraceComposition.lean) and [Foundations/TraceClosure](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/TraceClosure.lean), acyclic graphs [Foundations/ForwardValidity](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/ForwardValidity.lean); the packet-specific graph syntax, its well-formedness checks and the code-level compiler are not formalized. `thm:escape` (i) and (ii) are `existential_sealing` of [Foundations/StructuralRules](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/StructuralRules.lean) and `out_exists`/`out_unique` of [Foundations/ObservedRelation](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/ObservedRelation.lean); (iii) is a syntactic fact. "Finite strategy completeness, schema": the soundness half is `strategy_sound`/`bounded_strategy` of [Foundations/StrategyRealization](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/StrategyRealization.lean); completeness (from a formula to a strategy) is not derived. "Diameter composition" is `diameter_composition` of [Foundations/ObserverGuards](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/ObserverGuards.lean). `thm:open-bridge` ("Proposed open Foundation-to-Concise transport") is not derived: there is no Foundation-library bridge in this repository. |
| [foundations/STRIVE-iPRA-framework-2026-11-16](../docs/proofs/foundations/STRIVE-iPRA-framework-2026-11-16/) | [Foundations/MixedRadix](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/MixedRadix.lean), [Foundations/MixedRadixExpansion](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/MixedRadixExpansion.lean), [Foundations/DisjointSums](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/DisjointSums.lean); the append interval, the packed-code bound, mixed-radix decoding, the forward direction of code extensionality and disjoint decoding are checked; boundary composition, certificate sealing, bounded-formula compilation, the demand-closure instance, viability, observation erasure and the finite-net schema are not derived. Statements of this packet already derived under other packets: the Booleanization theorem (bounded-formula compilation, clauses (6.1) and (6.2) only) is the bounded-formula compiler of [Foundations/BoundedFormulas](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/BoundedFormulas.lean) with [Foundations/BooleanizationCorollaries](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/BooleanizationCorollaries.lean); the viability/path theorem (Theorem "Viability correctness") is [Foundations/BackwardViability](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/BackwardViability.lean), [Foundations/ViableChain](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/ViableChain.lean) and [Foundations/ChainViability](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/ChainViability.lean) for chains presented by terms, without the bounded existential over coded paths. Boundary composition and local certificate sealing are checked in [Foundations/StructuralRules](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/StructuralRules.lean). The finite-net optimization schema is the approximate-witness theorem of [Foundations/ApproximateWitness](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/ApproximateWitness.lean). Statements derived under other modules (each checked against the statement, 19 November 2026): "Finite monotone demand closure" is `capped_closure`, `capped_closure_least`, `iter_le_prefixed` of [Foundations/FiniteStabilization](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/FiniteStabilization.lean) and `demand_closure` of [Foundations/DemandClosure](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/DemandClosure.lean) (packed vectors from the zero seed); "Quotient observation principle" is `quotient_erasure`/`sealed_consumer` of [Foundations/ObserverErasure](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/ObserverErasure.lean) (bounded certificates); "STRIVE bounded-formula compilation" (6.1)–(6.7) is [Foundations/StriveCompilation](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/StriveCompilation.lean), with the canonical one-point certificates. |

`cor:lyapunov` is `lyapunov` there (a certified bound `α^k ≥ E[X^k]` gives `α^i ≥ E[X^i]` for `i ≤ k`, the natural-exponent case `r = i < s = k`); the regular-real root evaluators of the general statement are not formalized. `thm:vitali` (finite core: the truncation with separate tails for `X_n` and `X`) and `cor:bounded-convergence` are `vitali` and `bounded_convergence` of [Probability/VitaliEstimate](Lean/IPRADerivations/Probability/VitaliEstimate.lean), and the `L¹` modulus of `thm:dct` (each of the threshold, the probability rate and the tail at most a quarter of `2^{−r}`, uniformly for `n ≥ N`) is `dct_modulus` of [Probability/DominatedConvergence](Lean/IPRADerivations/Probability/DominatedConvergence.lean); the passage to effective `L¹` names is metalevel. `thm:quantum` (realness of `⟨A⟩`, `ψ*A²ψ = ‖Aψ‖²` and the variance identity `‖(A − ⟨A⟩)ψ‖² = ψ*A²ψ − ⟨A⟩²`, cleared of the normalization) is [Probability/QuantumExpectation](Lean/IPRADerivations/Probability/QuantumExpectation.lean) over integer terms in a fixed dimension, including the sign `(ΔA)² ≥ 0` for normalized `ψ` (`quantum_uncertainty`). The finite grid form of `thm:stieltjes` (a grid CDF `F` and a piecewise-linear `g`, with the increments `a(n) = F(n) − F(n−1)`) is the summation by parts `abel_parts` of [AnalyticNumberTheory/AbelParts](Lean/IPRADerivations/AnalyticNumberTheory/AbelParts.lean), `Σ_{n≤N} g(n) a(n) = F(N) g(N+1) − Σ_{n≤N} F(n)(g(n+1) − g(n))` with signed increments split in ℕ; the passage to improper limits is metalevel. Partly derived: `thm:compact-integral` (the refinement estimate `refine_upper`, `refine_lower` of [Analysis/RiemannRefinement](Lean/IPRADerivations/Analysis/RiemannRefinement.lean)), the finite identity of `prop:geometric` (`geometric_identity` of [Probability/GeometricMean](Lean/IPRADerivations/Probability/GeometricMean.lean)) and its tail bound `q^{dm} ≤ 2^{−m}` (`half_bound`, `power_half` of [Probability/GeometricTail](Lean/IPRADerivations/Probability/GeometricTail.lean)) with the explicit stage `N = dm`, `(N + 1) q^N ≤ 2^{−r}` (`geometric_cutoff` of [Probability/GeometricCutoff](Lean/IPRADerivations/Probability/GeometricCutoff.lean)). |
