## Verification

At the source based on `3c9d1097b3`, including the complete finite nonmonic
shrinking-step theorem, the root build completed 2,351 jobs and emitted
9,265 axiom reports for 8,756 distinct declarations. Every report uses only
`propext`, `Classical.choice` and `Quot.sound`. All nine import closure gates
passed: 3,770 modules in the portable arithmetic closure and 5,252 in the
independent `Diophantine` closure.

The six new modules contain 1,151 lines, 112 public theorems and 144 public
declarations, each with a fully qualified axiom probe. Independent full
source/math reviews checked the original coefficient apartness, derived
nonconstancy, actual selected coefficient representations, native and
normalized balanced-radius comparisons, and all program fields. They
checked that only genuine decoded denominator squares are cancelled;
selected-norm positivity is not an extra input, and zero residuals and
radius numerators are covered.
Actual center-to-starting radius transport and both displacement branches
were checked. Two independent critical reviews accepted the complete
source step-bound assembly and its 15-variable closed input/output maps.
The power calculation includes zero decay numerators and zero iteration
counter; it cancels only proved positive denominator powers.

`GaussianNonmonicStepBound.source_step_bound_closed` now proves the finite
source travel estimate r0<=U*C0*rho^j, with C0=B0*N*2^N/lambda, for the actual
starting balanced radius. `source_displacement_bound_closed` and
`source_displacement_program_bound_closed` prove the same bound for the
unchanged constructed displacement and its actual PR3 query. The input
has original polynomial shape, arbitrary positive-degree coefficient
apartness lambda=L/M>0, U=A/B>=1, the original point disk, Q<=rho^N,
C0>=1 and the actual residual bound B0*Q^j. Nonconstancy, maximum selection,
balanced-radius comparisons and the final native power budget are all
derived internally. No monicity, nonzero-leading-coefficient or canonical
original-input restriction is added. The source disk U-1 is relaxed to U,
and the finite estimate supports rho in [0,1]. Zero residuals are included.

`GaussianNonmonicTravelCoefficientBound` supplies the actual native
coefficient/radius inequality and derives nonconstancy from the original
apartness guard. `GaussianNonmonicTravelComparison` binds its literal
normalized rational operands and actual returned index/radius/values.
`GaussianTravelResidualClearing` specializes the true residual budget,
while `RationalShrinkingStepPowerBound` raises and compares the internal
powers and reflects their order. `GaussianNonmonicDisplacementRadius`
connects the selected center radius to the actual starting radius, with
no polynomial-shape or RD premise. The preceding uniform nonmonic RD,
finite approximate-root and concrete monic root-support/adequacy results
remain checked at their unchanged actual programs.

The general coefficient-name construction now retains every supplied
coefficient, including an undecided or zero leading coefficient. The original
h+2 apartness test derives the whole-tail lambda lower bound. All-coefficient
Horner perturbation includes the changing leading sample. Actual source
constants and finite Bernoulli/block estimates discharge all rate and clock
budgets. The actual finite product is uniformly bounded by the computed R
using a complementary-gap QF induction; no global product law is assumed.
The changing-precision PR4 trajectory uses P_j sampled at j+L, with derived
updates, canonicality, sampled nonconstancy and initial B residual. The computed
sampled-step theorem discharges every finite travel obligation except the
legitimate current point-disk and residual hypotheses.
The adjacent-sample drift theorem derives B Q^j/(8D) throughout the
computed R disk from coefficient coherence and the actual precision budget,
including the changing leading coefficient.

The actual residual recurrence and position-margin update now establish a
source-only simultaneous invariant by genuine QF induction. The next disk is
proved before the residual step uses coefficient drift. Actual finite gd
telescoping and the computed J/M schedules prove the two original general-FTA
main inequalities, including every later original coefficient sample and the
unchanged upper degree N. The actual returned root program is pointCN and lies
in R. Actual same-precision V_t uses all original coefficient observations and
alpha_t; its derived (2S+1/4)2^-t bound and bl(4S+1) shift construct a canonical
fast polynomial-value zero name. No global invariant, root-name validity,
polynomial-value coherence or zero-name law is a source premise.

The [general polynomial observer](Lean/IPRADerivations/Analysis/GaussianGeneralPolynomialEvaluation.lean)
now works at any bounded fast Gaussian point name, with separate numerical
coefficient and point data. All N+1 coefficients contribute to its derived
modulus (N+1)U^N plus the actual weighted Horner Lipschitz sum. Degree zero
and zero leading coefficients are included. The
[general value-name program](Lean/IPRADerivations/Analysis/GaussianGeneralPolynomialValueName.lean)
is canonical and fast from those source contracts alone. An arbitrary raw
K*2^-s envelope implies its zero predicate by finite fixed-query slack removal;
no comparison between K and the normalization modulus is assumed.
[The actual general FTA adapter](Lean/IPRADerivations/Analysis/GaussianGeneralFTAValueZero.lean)
derives that envelope at the returned root and proves zero of this same
generic polynomial-value program. Its original raw observations are identified;
the independently shifted normalized programs are not equated.

The [constant coefficient-name compiler](Lean/IPRADerivations/Analysis/GaussianConstantCoefficientNames.lean)
uses one fixed PR3 program and numerical data (N,C). It reads the original
descending entry C[N-i], proves coefficient coherence without a premise,
and computes dyadic apartness from the actual positive decoded denominator
and a nonzero native coefficient norm. Its closed source-input theorem needs
only 1<=k<=N and that nonzero coefficient. The
[original-polynomial frontend](Lean/IPRADerivations/Analysis/GaussianConstantPolynomialEvaluation.lean)
now proves entry equality and a genuine guarded QF Horner-prefix equality;
list length gives exact evaluation of the original numerical polynomial.
Its fixed root/value programs have proved pointCN and zero predicates, and
the original raw polynomial evaluations at the actual computed root precision
are bounded by 2^-t. Only length and one nonzero positive-degree coefficient
are supplied. Equality of arbitrary outer list codes is not asserted.

The [actual Euclidean name relation](Lean/IPRADerivations/Analysis/GaussianNameEquivalence.lean)
now has diagonal/cross-precision equivalence, equivalence laws on proper
names and actual program-zero transport. Every auxiliary precision is
removed by the proved finite integer-gap query. The
[independent normalizer theorem](Lean/IPRADerivations/Analysis/GaussianValueNormalizerExtensionality.lean)
keeps both numerical moduli, persistent data and actual precision shifts;
a vanishing raw discrepancy gives cross-equivalent normalized programs,
including zero moduli and without a modulus-order assumption.
[General polynomial extensionality](Lean/IPRADerivations/Analysis/GaussianGeneralPolynomialExtensionality.lean)
derives that discrepancy from equivalent original coefficient and point
names on a computed common proof disk. All N+1 coefficients participate.
The output programs retain their own original disks, data and shifts, and
their zero predicates agree. Degree zero and zero leading samples are
included. The [actual degree-padding program](Lean/IPRADerivations/Analysis/GaussianPolynomialDegreePadding.lean)
retains original coefficients through N and returns literal zero above it.
Two guarded QF Horner-prefix inductions prove exact padded evaluation for
N<=P, including degree zero. The
[different-degree comparison](Lean/IPRADerivations/Analysis/GaussianPolynomialDegreeExtensionality.lean)
compares two independently normalized original value programs through their
actual extensions at N+M. Their original disks, data and shifts are retained;
equivalent padded coefficient names and point names give equivalent values
and zero predicates. No outer list-code equality is used. Completed-field
ring/evaluation realization remains separate.

The [natural scalar action](Lean/IPRADerivations/Analysis/GaussianRationalNaturalScale.lean)
and [actual finite Gaussian sum](Lean/IPRADerivations/Analysis/GaussianRationalFiniteSum.lean)
now have canonical-output, zero/unit, recurrence, shifted-first-term,
bounded-entry congruence, addition and multiplication transport proofs.
The sum laws use genuine QF prefix inductions over the actual generated
lists; raw entries retain their canonical recoding where needed. These
provide the finite algebra for the now-proved
[inverse-binomial row](Lean/IPRADerivations/Analysis/GaussianInverseBinomialRows.lean).
Actual finite-sum Pascal recurrences and compiled bounded backward induction
derive every row. The [literal source identity](Lean/IPRADerivations/Analysis/GaussianRationalInverseTranslation.lean)
then binds ordinary binomial coefficients and the actual translated list:
the canonical recode of original coefficient a_k is
sum_{r=0}^{N-k} binom(k+r,k) b_{k+r} (-z)^r.
Only original list length and k<=N are assumed; N=0 and raw noncanonical
entries are supported. The actual PR4 output returns both equal quantities
and the translated list. Source reconciliation and the remaining packet
claims are not inferred from this identity.

The [coded finite-chain producer](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/CodedViableChain.lean)
stores the actual h+1 candidates and derives its object-language admissibility
and an independent PR bound on the code. A genuine QF induction checks any
numerical admissible code in the converse direction, yielding bounded coded
existence iff viability at the supplied start. The
[automatic initial selector](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/ViableChainSelection.lean)
then finds the least viable start and returns a success-tagged chain or the
literal failure output. Its success bit is equivalent to bounded existence
of any admissible full-horizon code; the entire tagged output has a proved
bound depending only on candidate bounds and horizon. No host admissibility,
successful search or output-law premise is supplied.
[Arbitrary-level coded suffixes](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/CodedViableSuffix.lean)
now prove the source equivalence for every numerical i<=h and a<B(i):
V(h-i,a)=1 iff there is a bounded admissible coded suffix starting at a.
The actual PR4 iterator and PR3 code output have proved length, start,
lookups and admissibility, with a separate PR2 bound depending only on B,
horizon and starting level. Both directions use genuine QF invariants;
the converse accepts arbitrary numerical codes.
[Coded stronger-evidence transport](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/CodedChainRefinement.lean)
now derives validity of the same numerical code from bounded object formulas
comparing sg-normalized local and edge tests. Shared candidate bounds and
horizon preserve the actual code bound; produced/returned chains, bounded
existence and the success bit transfer. Actual PR3/PR4 bounded products
conjoin an internal number of test requirements and derive every indexed
requirement on the decoded chain, including the empty product. Existence
under added constraints is not inferred. The remaining worked-example
claims still require separate proofs.

The [computed Gaussian-name disk](Lean/IPRADerivations/Analysis/GaussianNameBounds.lean)
is ceil(L1(f(0,x)))+2. The original Euclidean point-Cauchy condition alone
derives a uniform bound on every actual sample, without a supplied disk or
canonical-input premise. Its PR1 radius and PR2 sample/radius output have
proved returned-field and norm bounds.
[Actual Gaussian-name addition and multiplication](Lean/IPRADerivations/Analysis/GaussianNameArithmetic.lean)
now operate on the original samples with separate packed data. Their raw
regularity moduli, two and the sum of the computed input disks, follow from
the original point-Cauchy conditions alone. The actual normalized PR2 output
programs are canonical fast names. Equivalent original inputs produce
cross-equivalent outputs even when computed disks and normalization shifts
differ. Actual raw algebra and finite-slack transport derive these claims;
no output regularity or disk invariant is supplied.
[The actual name ring laws](Lean/IPRADerivations/Analysis/GaussianNameRingLaws.lean)
now derive associativity and commutativity for both operations, both
distributions, zero/one identities and additive inverses up to exact
cross-precision equivalence. Actual constant and normalized negative-name
programs are fast, with proved negation properness. A proved finite envelope
comparison removes arbitrary diagonal constants, so every nested computed
normalization shift is retained. Final ring-law hypotheses are only the
original point-Cauchy conditions.
[The actual reciprocal-name program](Lean/IPRADerivations/Analysis/GaussianNameReciprocal.lean)
now uses the original finite h+2 dyadic apartness witness. Point CN and that
witness derive tail separation, fractional reciprocal bounds and the
computed R-squared modulus. The actual output is canonical and fast; both
actual multiplication programs give the one-name class, and equivalent
inputs give equivalent inverses even with different apartness witnesses
and shifts. No apartness precision is selected from bare negated zero.
[Uniform polynomial-name Horner interpretation](Lean/IPRADerivations/Analysis/GaussianPolynomialNameHorner.lean)
uses one actual PR2 prefix program with numerical prefix length, original
degree and coefficient/point data. The original computed T bounds every
valid prefix; properness and the actual add/multiply recurrence are derived.
The empty prefix is equivalent to zero, and the full prefix retains the
original generic value program's degree, data, disk and shift, including
its exact zero predicate. A computed point disk removes the external disk
premise. The [collected name-level FTA certificate](Lean/IPRADerivations/Analysis/GaussianRepresentedFTA.lean)
now derives the actual trajectory root's point CN, original R disk, every
valid prefix's properness and arithmetic recurrence, and the full Horner
value's program-zero and exact zero-name class from the original source
input alone. The original coefficient data, root data, R, T and shifts are
retained. Its actual PR4 sample output returns the root, value and radius
with derived bounds and canonicality; the nonconstant coefficient index
is proof-only. No RD, root/disk/value/prefix/output law is supplied.
Completed-field realization and represented factorization remain separate.

[The actual stored filtered list](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/FilteredEnumerationCode.lean)
now encodes all increasing rank/select outputs in the checked sequence
format. Its PR2 producer and decoder have proved length, retained bounded
entries, completeness and strict order. Genuine QF inductions prove the
count is at most the original numerical cutoff and bound the actual code
by a separate PR2 pairing-stack bound, including empty cutoffs and lists.
These conclusions use no selector/list/output-law premise. Converting a
general height certificate into a numerical code cutoff remains separate.

The square-root worked example now has an
[actual stored candidate list](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/SquareRootRelationalCandidates.lean)
at exactly the source cutoff ceil((u/v+1)2^n)+1. Its computed predecessor of
the least crossing is retained, all returned rational candidates satisfy
the original (2u/v+3)2^-n residual, and consecutive computed witnesses
satisfy the original 3*2^-n compatibility. The source proof's strict upper
bracket is corrected to a nonstrict auxiliary bracket at perfect squares;
the residual and nonemptiness conclusions are unchanged.
The [finite-horizon certificate](Lean/IPRADerivations/Foundations/SquareRootRelationalCertificate.lean)
uses actual list indices and derives an object-admissible witness code from
v>=1 alone. The genuine backward viability computation then proves success
of its independent automatic selector. Its actual PR3 output has a verified
length-h+1 chain, original rational residual and compatibility at every
valid returned query, and independent chain/tag size bounds. All input
numerators, denominators, horizons and levels are internal; no successful
search, path or viability premise is supplied. Infinite branches and
selection from arbitrary represented input remain separate.
Generic completed Gaussian-point/field realization, coded-field norm/fiber/
annihilator claims, Minkowski physical-basis transport, remaining FIBER
compiler/adapters, Gamma/Barnes analytic identities and all other source
packet obligations remain in scope. Completed-real direction interpretation,
general convolution and the separately expanded telescoping sum remain
unclaimed. The complete 115-packet goal
remains active.

The new modules are included in `IPRADerivations`; all their public declarations
are audited in `Checks/Axioms.lean`. Run the root verification commands:

```sh
lake build IPRADerivations +IPRADerivations.Checks.Axioms
lake env lean --run Logic/PrimitiveRecursiveArithmetic/Bridge/check-import-closure.lean
```
